private SaslServer createSaslKerberosServer(final AuthenticateCallbackHandler saslServerCallbackHandler, final Map<String, ?> configs, Subject subject) throws IOException {\\n214:         // server is using a JAAS-authenticated subject: determine service principal name and hostname from kafka server's subject.\\n215:         final String servicePrincipal = SaslClientAuthenticator.firstPrincipal(subject);\\n216:         KerberosName kerberosName;\\n217:         try {\\n218:             kerberosName = KerberosName.parse(servicePrincipal);\\n219:         }  * Evaluates client responses via `SaslServer.evaluateResponse` and returns the issued challenge to the client until\\n237:      * authentication succeeds or fails.\\n238:      *\\n239:      * The messages are sent and received as size delimited bytes that consists of a 4 byte network-ordered size N\\n240:      * followed by N bytes representing the opaque payload.\\n241:      */\\n242:     @SuppressWarnings(\\\"fallthrough\\\")\\n243:     @Override\\n244:     public void authenticate() throws IOException {\\n245:         if (saslState != SaslState.REAUTH_PROCESS_HANDSHAKE) {\\n246:             if (netOutBuffer != null && !flushNetOutBufferAndUpdateInterestOps())\\n247:                 return;\\n248: \\n249:             if (saslServer != null && saslServer.isComplete()) {\\n250:                 setSaslState(SaslState.COMPLETE);\\n251:                 return;\\n252:             }\\n253: \\n254:             // allocate on heap (as opposed to any socket server memory pool)\\n255:             if (netInBuffer == null) netInBuffer = new NetworkReceive(MAX_RECEIVE_SIZE, connectionId);\\n256: \\n257:             netInBuffer.readFrom(transportLayer);\\n258:             if (!netInBuffer.complete())\\n259:                 return;\\n260:             netInBuffer.payload().rewind();\\n261:         }\\n262:         byte[] clientToken = new byte[netInBuffer.payload().remaining()];\\n263:         netInBuffer.payload().get(clientToken, 0, clientToken.length);\\n264:         netInBuffer = null; // reset the networkReceive as we read all the data.\\n265:         try {\\n266:             switch (saslState) {\\n267:                 case REAUTH_PROCESS_HANDSHAKE:\\n268:                 case HANDSHAKE_OR_VERSIONS_REQUEST:\\n269:                 case HANDSHAKE_REQUEST:\\n270:                     handleKafkaRequest(clientToken);\\n271:                     break;\\n272:                 case REAUTH_BAD_MECHANISM:\\n273:                     throw new SaslAuthenticationException(reauthInfo.badMechanismErrorMessage);\\n274:                 case INITIAL_REQUEST:\\n275:                     if (handleKafkaRequest(clientToken))\\n276:                         break;\\n277:                     // For default GSSAPI, fall through to authenticate using the client token as the first GSSAPI packet.\\n278:                     // This is required for interoperability with 0.9.0.x clients which do not send handshake request\\n279:                 case AUTHENTICATE:\\n280:                     handleSaslToken(clientToken);\\n281:                     // When the authentication exchange is complete and no more tokens are expected from the client,\\n282:                     // update SASL state. Current SASL state will be updated when outgoing writes to the client complete.\\n283:                     if (saslServer.isComplete())\\n284:                         setSaslState(SaslState.COMPLETE);\\n285:                     break;\\n286:                 default:\\n287:                     break;\\n288:             }\\n289:         } catch (AuthenticationException e) {\\n290:             // Exception will be propagated after response is sent to client\\n291:             setSaslState(SaslState.FAILED, e);\\n292:         } catch (Exception e) {\\n293:             // In the case of IOExceptions and other unexpected exceptions, fail immediately\\n294:             saslState = SaslState.FAILED;\\n295:             LOG.debug(\\\"Failed during {}: {}\\\", reauthInfo.authenticationOrReauthenticationText(), e.getMessage());\\n296:             throw e;\\n297:         }\\n298:     }\\n299: \\n300:     @Override\\n301:     public KafkaPrincipal principal() {\\n302:         Optional<SSLSession> sslSession = transportLayer instanceof SslTransportLayer ?\\n303:                 Optional.of(((SslTransportLayer) transportLayer).sslSession()) : Optional.empty();\\n304:         SaslAuthenticationContext context = new SaslAuthenticationContext(saslServer, securityProtocol,\\n305:                 clientAddress(), listenerName.value(), sslSession);\\n306:         KafkaPrincipal principal = principalBuilder.build(context);\\n307:         if (ScramMechanism.isScram(saslMechanism) && Boolean.parseBoolean((String) saslServer.getNegotiatedProperty(ScramLoginModule.TOKEN_AUTH_CONFIG))) {\\n308:             principal.tokenAuthenticated(true);\\n309:         }\\n310:         return principal;\\n311:     }\\n312: \\n313:     @Override\\n314:     public Optional<KafkaPrincipalSerde> principalSerde() {\\n315:         return principalBuilder instanceof KafkaPrincipalSerde ? SaslState.COMPLETE;\\n321:     }\\n322: \\n323:     @Override\\n324:     public void handleAuthenticationFailure() throws IOException {\\n325:         sendAuthenticationFailureResponse();\\n326:     }\\n327: \\n328:     @Override\\n329:     public void close() throws IOException {\\n330:         if (principalBuilder instanceof Closeable)\\n331:             Utils.closeQuietly((Closeable)    return transportLayer.socketChannel().socket().getInetAddress();\\n409:     }\\n410: \\n411:     private void handleSaslToken(byte[] clientToken) throws IOException {\\n412:         if             if (!apiKey.isVersionSupported(version)) {\\n437:                 // We cannot create an error response if the request version of SaslAuthenticate is not supported\\n438:                 // This should not normally occur since clients typically check supported versions using ApiVersionsRequest\\n439:                 throw new UnsupportedVersionException(\\\"Version \\\" + version + \\n492:     private boolean handleKafkaRequest(byte[] requestBytes) throws IOException, AuthenticationException {\\n493:         boolean isKafkaRequest = false;\\n494:         String clientMechanism = null;\\n495:         try {\\n496:             ByteBuffer requestBuffer = ByteBuffer.wrap(requestBytes);\\n497:             RequestHeader header = RequestHeader.parse(requestBuffer);\\n498:             ApiKeys apiKey = header.apiKey();\\n499: \\n500:             // A valid Kafka request header was received. SASL authentication tokens are now expected only\\n501:             // following a SaslHandshakeRequest since this is not a GSSAPI client token from a Kafka 0.9.0.x client.\\n502:             if (saslState == SaslState.INITIAL_REQUEST)\\n503:                 setSaslState(SaslState.HANDSHAKE_OR_VERSIONS_REQUEST);\\n504:             isKafkaRequest = true;\\n505: \\n506:             // Raise an error prior to parsing if the api cannot be handled at this layer. This avoids\\n507:             // unnecessary exposure to some of the more complex schema types.\\n508:             if (apiKey != ApiKeys.API_VERSIONS && apiKey != ApiKeys.SASL_HANDSHAKE)\\n509:                 throw new IllegalSaslStateException(\\\"Unexpected Kafka request of type \\\" + apiKey + \\\" during SASL handshake.\\\");\\n510: \\n511:             LOG.debug(\\\"Handling Kafka request {} during {}\\\", apiKey, reauthInfo.authenticationOrReauthenticationText());\\n512: \\n513: \\n514:             RequestContext requestContext = new RequestContext(header, connectionId, clientAddress(),\\n515:                     KafkaPrincipal.ANONYMOUS, listenerName, securityProtocol, ClientInformation.EMPTY, false);\\n516:             RequestAndSize requestAndSize = requestContext.parseRequest(requestBuffer);\\n517:             if (apiKey == ApiKeys.API_VERSIONS)\\n518:                 handleApiVersionsRequest(requestContext, (ApiVersionsRequest) requestAndSize.request);\\n519:             else\\n520:                 clientMechanism = handleHandshakeRequest(requestContext, (SaslHandshakeRequest) requestAndSize.request);\\n521:         } catch (InvalidRequestException e) {\\n522:             if (saslState == SaslState.INITIAL_REQUEST) {\\n523:                 // InvalidRequestException is thrown if the request is not in Kafka format or if the API key\\n524:                 // is invalid. For compatibility with 0.9.0.x where the first packet is a GSSAPI token\\n525:                 // starting with 0x60, revert to GSSAPI for both these exceptions.\\n526:                 if (LOG.isDebugEnabled()) {\\n527:                     StringBuilder tokenBuilder = new StringBuilder();\\n528:                     for (byte b : requestBytes) {\\n529:                         tokenBuilder.append(String.format(\\\"%02x\\\", b));\\n530:                         if (tokenBuilder.length() >= 20)\\n531:                              break;\\n532:                     }\\n533:                     LOG.debug(\\\"Received client packet of length {} starting with bytes 0x{}, process as GSSAPI packet\\\", requestBytes.length, tokenBuilder);\\n534:                 }\\n535: UnsupportedSaslMechanismException(\\\"Exception handling first SASL packet from client, GSSAPI is not supported by server\\\", e);\\n540:             } else\\n541:                 throw e;\\n542: createSaslServer(clientMechanism);\\n546:             setSaslState(SaslState.AUTHENTICATE);\\n547:         }\\n548:         return isKafkaRequest;\\n549:     }\\n550: \\n551:     private String handleHandshakeRequest(RequestContext context, SaslHandshakeRequest handshakeRequest) throws IOException, UnsupportedSaslMechanismException {\\n552:         String clientMechanism = handshakeRequest.data().mechanism();\\n553:         short version = context.header.apiVersion();\\n554:         if (version >= 1)\\n555: }\\n546:   }\\n547: \\n548:   private def loadCache(): Unit = {\\n549:     lock synchronized  {\\n550:       ZkAclStore.stores.foreach(store => {\\n551:         val resourceTypes = }\\n619: \\n620:   /**\\n621:     * Safely updates the resources ACLs by ensuring reads and writes respect the expected zookeeper version.\\n622:     * Continues to retry until it successfully updates * @param getNewAcls function to transform existing acls to new ACLs\\n628:     * @return boolean indicating if a change was made\\n629:     */\\n630:   private def updateResourceAcls(resource: ResourcePattern)(getNewAcls: Set[AclEntry] => Set[AclEntry]): Boolean = {\\n631:     var currentVersionedAcls =\\n632:       if (aclCache.contains(resource))\\n633:
